([pedrohenriquesanchesleal4-debug/izanagi-ai](https://github.com/pedrohenriquesanchesleal4-debug/izanagi-ai/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/izanagi-senior-engineer))
- **izanagi-simulator**: Counterfactual simulation engine that generates 3 divergent alternate paths before committing to high-stakes plans, evaluating each by EV/risk/reversibility with explicit simulation records. Use when facing CRITICAL or HIGH complexity decisions with multiple viable paths, when the user says "simulate this decision", "what if we tried X instead", or "I need to evaluate alternatives before committing". ([nexuslinkproductions/yuri-os](https://github.com/nexuslinkproductions/yuri-os/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/izanagi-simulator) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **izanagi-skill-architect**: Ativa a persona e diretrizes especializadas do agente Skill Architect (Arquitetura de novas skills: Capability Gap → Research → Draft → Examples → Tests → Security Scan → Evaluation → Register (zero skills desnecessárias)). Use quando faltar uma skill comprovadamente necessária: cura duplicação e lacunas da biblioteca de skills. ([pedrohenriquesanchesleal4-debug/izanagi-ai](https://github.com/pedrohenriquesanchesleal4-debug/izanagi-ai/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/izanagi-skill-architect))
- **izanagi-techlead**: Ativa a persona e diretrizes especializadas do agente Tech Lead (Liderança técnica operacional, Code Review pedagógico em 5 dimensões (Corretude, Segurança, Performance, Manutenibilidade, DX), governança de padrões de código e desbloqueio de engenheiros). Use PROACTIVELY quando o pedido é revisão pedagógica de código/padrão (o "porquê" de uma mudança) ou governança de convenções: não para veredito de aprovar/reprovar. ([pedrohenriquesanchesleal4-debug/izanagi-ai](https://github.com/pedrohenriquesanchesleal4-debug/izanagi-ai/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/izanagi-techlead))
- **izanami**: Repeat a READ-ONLY task until a condition holds. Use when the maintainer invokes hatsu:izanami &lt;task&gt; until &lt;condition&gt;, or asks to watch, poll, or keep checking something until it changes. Observation only — it reads, reports and stops; it never writes to GitHub, never labels, never merges. For a loop that must act, use hatsu:izanagi, which requires an explicit iteration cap. ([zheref/hatsu](https://github.com/zheref/hatsu/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/izanami))
